2026-11-14T00:03:29.6310933Z 
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6311259Z # workflow_call path (called from integration.yml). The planner only
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6311785Z # invokes us when a build is actually needed (no pin/cache match), so
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6312293Z # we always publish. The publish mode follows the caller's event:
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6312842Z # pull_request -> pr-scoped image (consumed by the calling Integration
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6313376Z # run via digest output, not by tag polling); main/schedule/dispatch
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6313929Z # -> pin-refresh image (operator opens a reviewed image-pin.json PR
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6314333Z # afterwards).
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6314609Z if [[ -n "$EXPECTED_FINGERPRINT" ]]; then
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6314924Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6315344Z   if [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "pull_request" && "$PR_HEAD_REPO" == "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" ]]; then
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6316271Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pr"
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6316620Z     PR_IMAGE_TAG="${IMAGE_NAME}:pr-${PR_NUMBER}-${SHORT_SHA}"
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6317092Z   elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "push" && "$REF" == "refs/heads/main" ]]; then
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6317485Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6317889Z   elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "schedule" || "$EVENT_NAME" == "workflow_dispatch" ]]; then
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6318327Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6318573Z   else
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6318888Z     # Untrusted PR (fork) reaching workflow_call should have been
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6319345Z     # blocked at the planner; defend in depth here too.
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6319950Z     echo "::error::workflow_call invoked from unsupported event ($EVENT_NAME, head_repo=$PR_HEAD_REPO)." >&2
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6320483Z     exit 1
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6320694Z   fi
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6321031Z # Standalone path: push to main, weekly schedule, or manual dispatch.
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6321559Z # These refresh the reviewed image-pin.json digest. The pull_request
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6322073Z # trigger no longer exists on this workflow; PR builds reach us only
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6322505Z # through the workflow_call branch above.
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6322932Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "push" && "$REF" == "refs/heads/main" ]]; then
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6323308Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"

2026-11-14T00:03:29.6324365Z   PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6324654Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "workflow_dispatch" ]]; then

2026-11-14T00:03:29.6325316Z   if [[ "$DISPATCH_PUSH" == "true" ]]; then
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6325620Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6326063Z   fi
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6326260Z fi
2026-11-14T00:03:29.6326439Z {
